     1	import csv,json
     2	from decimal import Decimal
     3	from pathlib import Path
     4	
     5	def build(source,destination):
     6	    posted={}
     7	    with Path(source).open(newline='') as stream:
     8	        for row in csv.DictReader(stream):
     9	            if row['status']=='posted':

     6	    td=tempfile.mkdtemp();d=Path(td)
     7	    (d/'transactions.csv').write_text(source_text)
     8	    build(d/'transactions.csv',d/'report.json')
     9	    report=json.loads((d/'report.json').read_text())
    10	    reconciliation=json.loads((d/'reconciliation.json').read_text())
    11	    return report,reconciliation
    12	
    13	class ReportSmoke(unittest.TestCase):
    14	 def test_posted_total(self):
    15	  report,_=run('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\np,99.00,pending\na,0.10,posted\n')
    16	  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.30','rows':2})
    17	
    18	 def test_mixed_reconciliation(self):
    19	  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\na,0.10,posted\nc,10.00,pending\nd,-0.05,posted\n')
    20	  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.25','rows':3})
    21	  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':['a','b','d'],'excluded_pending_ids':['c'],'duplicate_ids':['a'],'total':'0.25'})
    22	
    23	 def test_empty_input(self):
    24	  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\n')
    25	  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.00','rows':0})
    26	  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':[],'excluded_pending_ids':[],'duplicate_ids':[],'total':'0.00'})
    27	
    28	 def test_pending_duplicate(self):
    29	  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\nx,5.00,posted\ny,5.00,posted\nz,1.00,pending\nz,2.00,pending\n')
    30	  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'10.00','rows':2})
    31	  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':['x','y'],'excluded_pending_ids':['z'],'duplicate_ids':['z'],'total':'10.00'})
    32	
    33	 def test_cli_produces_both_artifacts(self):
    34	  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    35	   d=Path(td)
    36	   (d/'transactions.csv').write_text('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\na,0.10,posted\nc,10.00,pending\nd,-0.05,posted\n')
    37	   (d/'report.py').write_text(Path('report.py').read_text())
    38	   env={'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PATH':'/usr/bin:/bin'}
    39	   subprocess.run([sys.executable,'report.py'],cwd=str(d),check=True,env=env)
    40	   self.assertEqual(json.loads((d/'report.json').read_text()),{'total':'0.25','rows':3})
    41	   self.assertEqual(json.loads((d/'reconciliation.json').read_text()),{'included_ids':['a','b','d'],'excluded_pending_ids':['c'],'duplicate_ids':['a'],'total':'0.25'})
    42	
    43	if __name__=='__main__':
    44	 unittest.main()
    45

===== report.py =====
import csv,json
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

def build(source,destination):
    posted={}
    pending=set()
    occurrences={}
    with Path(source).open(newline='') as stream:
        for row in csv.DictReader(stream):
            id_=row['id']
            occurrences[id_]=occurrences.get(id_,0)+1
            if row['status']=='posted':
                posted.setdefault(id_,Decimal(row['amount']))
            else:
                pending.add(id_)
    total=sum(posted.values(),Decimal('0'))
    report={'total':f'{total:.2f}','rows':len(posted)}
    Path(destination).write_text(json.dumps(report))
    reconciliation={
        'included_ids':sorted(posted),
        'excluded_pending_ids':sorted(pending),
        'duplicate_ids':sorted(id_ for id_,count in occurrences.items() if count>1),
        'total':report['total'],
    }
    Path(destination).with_name('reconciliation.json').write_text(json.dumps(reconciliation))

if __name__=='__main__':
    build('transactions.csv','report.json')

===== test_report.py =====
import subprocess,sys,tempfile,json,unittest,os
from pathlib import Path
from report import build

def run(source_text):
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        d=Path(td)
        (d/'transactions.csv').write_text(source_text)
        build(d/'transactions.csv',d/'report.json')
        report=json.loads((d/'report.json').read_text())
        reconciliation=json.loads((d/'reconciliation.json').read_text())
        return report,reconciliation

class ReportSmoke(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_posted_total(self):
  report,_=run('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\np,99.00,pending\na,0.10,posted\n')
  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.30','rows':2})

 def test_mixed_reconciliation(self):
  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\na,0.10,posted\nc,10.00,pending\nd,-0.05,posted\n')
  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.25','rows':3})
  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':['a','b','d'],'excluded_pending_ids':['c'],'duplicate_ids':['a'],'total':'0.25'})

 def test_empty_input(self):
  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\n')
  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'0.00','rows':0})
  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':[],'excluded_pending_ids':[],'duplicate_ids':[],'total':'0.00'})

 def test_pending_duplicate(self):
  report,reconciliation=run('id,amount,status\nx,5.00,posted\ny,5.00,posted\nz,1.00,pending\nz,2.00,pending\n')
  self.assertEqual(report,{'total':'10.00','rows':2})
  self.assertEqual(reconciliation,{'included_ids':['x','y'],'excluded_pending_ids':['z'],'duplicate_ids':['z'],'total':'10.00'})

 def test_cli_produces_both_artifacts(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   d=Path(td)
   (d/'transactions.csv').write_text('id,amount,status\na,0.10,posted\nb,0.20,posted\na,0.10,posted\nc,10.00,pending\nd,-0.05,posted\n')
   (d/'report.py').write_text(Path('report.py').read_text())
   env=dict(os.environ);env['PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE']='1'
   subprocess.run([sys.executable,'report.py'],cwd=str(d),check=True,env=env)
   self.assertEqual(json.loads((d/'report.json').read_text()),{'total':'0.25','rows':3})
   self.assertEqual(json.loads((d/'reconciliation.json').read_text()),{'included_ids':['a','b','d'],'excluded_pending_ids':['c'],'duplicate_ids':['a'],'total':'0.25'})

if __name__=='__main__':
 unittest.main()
